Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on stability structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 837. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 54 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by stability generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 837, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 54. QED.
